Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: rw_lab.exe execution from cmd.exe (PID 9363), file encryption to *.locked, ransom note creation, failed shadow deletion attempt, no persistence, no network activity. Unique defects: 1. Missing hash in chunk_0; 2. Unresolved evidence reference in chunk_1; 3. Material false claim about network activity in chunk_15; 4. Placeholder in required field (VM OS) in chunk_0; 5. Coarse timeline in chunk_14.
